var(--accent2));\n      color:var(--ctaText);\n      border-color:transparent;\n    }\n    .btnSecondary{\n      background:rgba(255,255,255,.04);\n      color:var(--text);\n    }\n    .btn:active{transform:translateY(1px)}\n    .btn:focus{outline:none; box-shadow:var(--focus)}\n\n    .microcopy{\n      margin:0;\n      color:var(--muted2);\n      font-size:12.5px;\n    }\n\n    .heroMedia{\n      border-top:1px solid var(--line);\n      background:rgba(0,0,0,.15);\n      padding:12px;\n    }\n    .heroMedia img{\n      width:100%;\n      height:auto;\n      display:block;\n      border-radius:12px;\n      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.10);\n      background:#0a0f17;\n    }\n\n    /* Sections */\n    main{padding:10px 0 90px}\n    section{padding:14px 0}\n    .sectionCard{\n      border:1px solid var(--line);\n      border-radius:var(--radius);\n      background:linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,255,255,.03), rgba(255,255,255,.015));\n      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.35);\n      overflow:hidden;\n    }\n    .sectionInner{padding:18px}\n    h2{margin:0 0 8px; font-size:20px; letter-spacing:-.2px}\n    .lead{margin:0 0 12px; color:var(--muted); font-size:14.5px}\n    .bullets{margin:0; padding-left:18px; color:var(--muted)}\n    .bullets li{margin:8px 0}\n\n    .inlineCta{margin-top:14px}\n\n    /* Form */\n    .formGrid{display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr; gap:12px}\n    .formTitle{margin:0 0 8px; font-size:18px}\n    form{margin-top:10px}\n    .field{display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:6px; margin:10px 0}\n    label{font-size:13px; color:var(--text); font-weight:700}\n    input, select{\n      width:100%;\n      padding:12px 12px;\n      border-radius:12px;\n      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14);\n      background:rgba(0,0,0,.28);\n      color:var(--text);\n      font-size:15px;\n    }\n    input::placeholder{color:rgba(185,196,214,.7)}\n    input:focus, select:focus{outline:none; box-shadow:var(--focus); border-color:rgba(96,165,250,.45)}\n    .help{margin:0; color:var(--muted2); font-size:12.5px}\n    .error{margin:8px 0 0; color:var(--danger); font-size:13px; display:none}\n    .success{\n      margin-top:10px;\n      padding:12px;\n      border-radius:12px;\n      border:1px solid rgba(52,211,153,.25);\n      background:rgba(52,211,153,.06);\n      color:var(--muted);\n      display:none;\n    }\n    .success b{color:var(--text)}\n\n    /* Timeline */\n    .timeline{display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr; gap:10px; margin-top:10px}\n    .tItem{\n      padding:12px;\n      border-radius:14px;\n      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12);\n      background:rgba(0,0,0,.22);\n    }\n    .tTop{display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:10px; margin-bottom:6px}\n    .tWeek{font-weight:900; color:var(--text); font-size:13px}\n    .tIcon{\n      width:28px; height:28px;\n      border-radius:10px;\n      display:grid; place-items:center;\n      background:rgba(45,212,191,.10);\n      border:1px solid rgba(45,212,191,.22);\n      color:var(--text);\n      font-weight:900;\n    }\n    .tText{margin:0; color:var(--muted); font-size:13.5px}\n    .note{\n      margin-top:12px;\n      padding:12px;\n      border-radius:12px;\n      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12);\n      background:rgba(255,255,255,.03);\n      color:var(--muted2);\n      font-size:12.5px;\n    }\n\n    /* Media blocks */\n    .mediaRow{display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr; gap:12px; align-items:start}\n    .mediaRow img{\n      width:100%; height:auto; display:block;\n      border-radius:14px;\n      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.10);\n      background:#0a0f17;\n    }\n\n    /* Compliance */\n    .compliance{\n      margin-top:12px;\n      padding:14px;\n      border-radius:14px;\n      border:1px solid rgba(96,165,250,.25);\n      background:rgba(96,165,250,.07);\n      color:var(--muted);\n      font-size:13px;\n    }\n    .compliance b{color:var(--text)}\n\n    /* FAQ */\n    .faq details{\n      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12);\n      background:rgba(0,0,0,.20);\n      border-radius:14px;\n      padding:12px;\n      margin:10px 0;\n    }\n    .faq summary{\n      cursor:pointer;\n      font-weight:900;\n      color:var(--text);\n      list-style:none;\n    }\n    .faq summary:focus{outline:none; box-shadow:var(--focus); border-radius:12px}\n    .faq p{margin:10px 0 0; color:var(--muted); font-size:14px}\n\n    /* Sticky CTA */\n    .sticky{\n      position:fixed;\n      left:0; right:0; bottom:0;\n      padding:10px 12px;\n      background:rgba(11,15,20,.85);\n      border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12);\n      backdrop-filter: blur(10px);\n      display:none;\n      z-index:1000;\n    }\n    .stickyInner{max-width:var(--max); margin:0 auto; display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center}\n    .stickyNote{display:none; color:var(--muted2); font-size:12px; line-height:1.2; max-width:34%}\n\n    /* Desktop tweaks */\n    @media (min-width: 920px){\n      h1{font-size:40px}\n      .heroGrid{grid-template-columns: 1.2fr .8fr; gap:16px}\n      .heroMedia{border-top:none; border-left:1px solid var(--line)}\n      .heroCard{display:grid; grid-template-columns: 1.15fr .85fr}\n      .formGrid{grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap:16px}\n @media (max-width: 919px){\n      .sticky.show{display:block}\n      .stickyNote{display:block}\n    }\n\n    /* Reduce motion */\n    @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){\n A estrutura é de comunicação + onboarding: deixar seu valor claro antes do preço e guiar as primeiras semanas com marcos e check-ins. Você ajusta a linguagem e os marcos para sua realidade.</p>\n            </details>\n\n            <details>\n              <summary>Eu já fecho alunos, mas eles somem nas primeiras semanas. Ajuda mesmo?</summary>\n              <p>A prévia foca exatamente no começo: marcos iniciais + check-ins para o aluno sentir cuidado e progresso rápido, o que tende a melhorar engajamento e reduzir o “sumiço” quando aplicado com consistência.</p>\n            </details>\n\n            <details>\n              <summary>Isso garante agenda cheia em até 8 semanas?</summary>\n              <p>Não existe garantia de resultado individual. “Em até 8 semanas” é a estrutura do processo sugerido (sequência de comunicação + marcos + check-ins), que costuma melhorar percepção de valor e engajamento quando bem aplicado.</p>\n            </details>\n          </div>\n\n          <div class=\"inlineCta\">\n            <a class=\"btn btnSecondary\" href=\"#desbloquear\" data-scroll>\n              Desbloquear o Kit (receber a prévia gerada por IA)\n            </a>\n            <p class=\"microcopy\" style=\"margin-top:10px\">Prévia do kit (IA): mensagens + roteiro + check-ins para começar hoje.</p>\n          </div>\n\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n\n  </main>\n\n  <!-- Sticky CTA (mobile) -->\n  <div class=\"sticky\" id=\"stickyCta\" role=\"region\" aria-label=\"CTA fixo\">\n    <div class=\"stickyInner\">\n      <div class=\"stickyNote\">Prévia do kit (IA): mensagens + roteiro + check-ins.</div>\n      <a class=\"btn btnPrimary\" href=\"#desbloquear\" data-scroll style=\"flex:1\">\n        Desbloquear o Kit (receber a prévia gerada por IA)\n      </a>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n  <script>\n    (function(){\n      // Smooth scroll for in-page CTAs\n      function scrollToId(hash){\n        var el = document.querySelector(hash);\n        if(!el) return;\n        el.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'});\n        // Move focus for accessibility after scrolling\n        setTimeout(function(){\n          var focusable = el.querySelector('input, select, button, a, summary');\n          if(focusable){ focusable.focus({preventScroll:true}); }\n        }, 450);\n      }\n\n      document.addEventListener('click', function(e){\n        var a = e.target.closest('a[data-scroll]');\n        if(!a) return;\n        var href = a.getAttribute('href');\n        if(href && href.startsWith('#')){\n          e.preventDefault();\n          history.replaceState(null,'',href);\n          scrollToId(href);\n        }\n      });\n\n      // Sticky CTA show after 25% scroll on mobile\n      var sticky = document.getElementById('stickyCta');\n      function onScroll(){\n        if(window.matchMedia('(min-width: 920px)').matches){\n          sticky.classList.remove('show');\n          return;\n        }\n        var doc = document.documentElement;\n        var scrolled = (doc.scrollTop || document.body.scrollTop);\n        var height = (doc.scrollHeight - doc.clientHeight) || 1;\n        var pct = scrolled / height;\n        if(pct > 0.25){ sticky.classList.add('show'); }\n        else{ sticky.classList.remove('show'); }\n      }\n      window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, {passive:true});\n      onScroll();\n\n      // Form validation (required: nome, objetivo; whatsapp optional but if filled, validate basic)\n      var form = document.getElementById('leadForm');\n      var nome = document.getElementById('nome');\n      var whatsapp = document.getElementById('whatsapp');\n      var objetivo = document.getElementById('objetivo');\n      var error = document.getElementById('formError');\n      var success = document.getElementById('formSuccess');\n      var submitBtn = document.getElementById('submitBtn');\n\n      function isValidName(v){ return (v || '').trim().length >= 2; }\n      function isValidGoal(v){ return (v || '').trim().length > 0; }\n      function cleanPhone(v){ return (v || '').replace(/\\D/g,''); }\n      function isValidPhoneOptional(v){\n        var c = cleanPhone(v);\n        if(c.length === 0) return true;\n        // Brazil patterns: 10 or 11 digits (DDD + number)\n        return (c.length === 10 || c.length === 11);\n      }\n\n      function setInvalid(el, invalid){\n        if(invalid){\n          el.setAttribute('aria-invalid','true');\n          el.style.borderColor = 'rgba(251,113,133,.75)';\n        } else {\n          el.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');\n          el.style.borderColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,.14)';\n        }\n      }\n\n      form.addEventListener('submit', function(e){\n        e.preventDefault();\n        error.style.display = 'none';\n        success.style.display = 'none';\n\n        var okNome = isValidName(nome.value);\n        var okObj = isValidGoal(objetivo.value);\n        var okWpp = isValidPhoneOptional(whatsapp.value);\n\n        setInvalid(nome, !okNome);\n        setInvalid(objetivo, !okObj);\n        setInvalid(whatsapp, !okWpp);\n\n        if(!okNome || !okObj || !okWpp){\n          error.style.display = 'block';\n          error.textContent = (!okWpp && cleanPhone(whatsapp.value).length>0)\n            ? 'WhatsApp parece inválido. Use DDD + número (10 ou 11 dígitos).'\n            : 'Confira os campos obrigatórios: Nome e Objetivo principal.';\n          // Focus first invalid field\n          if(!okNome) nome.focus();\n          else if(!okObj) objetivo.focus();\n          else whatsapp.focus();\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // Simulated success (integration point: replace with HubSpot form handler)\n        submitBtn.disabled = true;\n        submitBtn.style.opacity = '0.9';\n        submitBtn.textContent = 'Enviando…';\n\n        setTimeout(function(){\n          submitBtn.disabled = false;\n          submitBtn.textContent = 'Desbloquear o Kit (receber a prévia gerada por IA)';\n          success.style.display = 'block';\n          // Keep values (often helpful for tracking); could clear if desired\n        }, 650);\n      });\n\n      // If URL has #desbloquear on load, focus the form\n      window.addEventListener('load', function(){\n        if(window.location.hash === '#desbloquear'){\n          setTimeout(function(){\n            var el = document.querySelector('#desbloquear');\n            if(el){\n              el.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'});\n              setTimeout(function(){ nome.focus({preventScroll:true}); }, 500);\n            }\n          }, 150);\n        }\n      });\n\n    })();\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
  }
}
